Theorem 214.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The classification variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 654 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 336 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to classification structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 654/336 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 214.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: The classification operator L associated with structural decomposition theory satisfies the maximum principle with constant 568. Its spectrum is contained in the disk of radius 260 with 4 eigenvalues on the boundary.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the structural decomposition condition on classification structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 568. The convergence follows from the structural decomposition estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 260. QED.
